| TS_001 | Given an inbound EDI 834 file, if the member’s eligibility span has not yet begun, the Eligibility Date (DTP*356/DTP*348) populates as the transaction effective date. | Verify AR PASSE new enrollment uses future Eligibility Date as Facets transaction effective date | TC_TS001_001 | None | Positive | Functional | Validate that an inbound AR PASSE 834 new enrollment for a member whose eligibility has not yet begun loads successfully and stores the transaction effective date from the future Eligibility Date in DTP*356 or DTP*348 instead of the earlier Assessment Date. | AR PASSE enrollment test data is available for one new member with no existing subscriber, member, or eligibility record in the target certification environment before execution.
